**Status kinds:**

- **lb-tested**: probe # and public score.
- **lab-tested**: local numbers.
- **refuted**: one-line reason. This includes explicit closures, rejected proposals and items made MOOT (e.g. a pre-registered branch whose trigger did not occur).
- **open-untested**.
- **deferred-after-deadline**: post-29 Sep, or blocked by rule R8/R9.
- **in-plan**: slot/gate. 'APPLIED', 'RECORDED' or 'DONE' marks a finished ops item.

## LOC - Localisation / coordinate head (V1284 coordhead, float output, lowrefine, head retrain)

Impact: Only in-window lever with a public proof: +0.002 on our base (probe 31), +0.007 inside x138 (CONTEXT.md l.1495-1504). lowrefine scored neutral (d1 = d2 = 0) and stays in both finals. Round 8: a constant +0.1 vox z shift gains +0.0016 in-sample on the x138 lab but ~+0.0003 transfer-adjusted -> post-deadline (I-029); post-deadline also axial localisation of z-elongated 6bba nuclei (I-261). Curation 18: coord-inject (I-324) is built as exp75 (dummy stuck in the Kaggle queue since 13:19Z) and rebased as exp76a for W1 (I-374): in-sample 4-clip lab +0.002..+0.003, P(display >= 0.955) ~0.35-0.40, public-only; nearest transfer record Eric sub-voxel +0.0078 lab -> -0.002 LB (research/wf_ideas_v4/round18/report.json R18-A1 (5), new_facts (10)). <a id="div"></a>
## DIV - Divisions (safe-division gates, DivNet, DaughterNet insertion, forkmid/forkblob, DeepCenter threshold)

Impact: 0.1 x div_J; public div_J <= 0.36 (probe 26); realised +0.001 twice (probes 28, 35), insertion is inside K2. The fork-REMOVAL family is exhausted on exp60 (forkmid, forkblob, ANTIPAR FAIL; DNVETO dropped; K3b FAIL) (CONTEXT.md l.1918-1937). NEW 27 Sep: the DeepCenter safe-division threshold is the one division knob with a strong public signal (0.25 -> 0.30 = -0.005, lab said +0.001; CONTEXT.md l.2789-2799): one public division TP ~0.0015-0.0023 display, so 'division knobs are invisible on public' is wrong for the DC band and the exp60 lab cannot sign fork-removal knobs (research/wf_ideas_v4/round15/report.json R15-A6 (5), (6); I-347, I-348). Probe 42 (fm45) = 0.954 with an attributable UP: the first fork-removal knob with a positive public read (one embryo, inside the bin). Round 16: forkblob measures +0.00019 on the K2-like ins35 bed (undecidable), so removal gates belong on the shipped stack (I-359, post-deadline).

<a id="nodes"></a>
## NODES - Node budget & recall (track length, threshold, pruning, readmit/gapfill, hole fill, leaf prune)

Impact: Node multiplier is linear (0.001 per 1% nodes); every LB node-count lever was <= 0 except leaf prune (tie, probes 27 and 36) (CONTEXT.md l.1169-1171; experiments/RESULTS.md l.69). Round 11: the whole missing-node pool on x138 caps at +0.029 lab (1,211 FN edge-ends / 41,774), 80% multi-frame runs, mostly peakless in the raw image; a raw-maxima recall arm caps at +0.0059 (I-293); dropping nodes for the bonus costs ~21x its gain (research/wf_ideas_v4/round11/scan_hyp_detect.json r11d-1, r11d-2, r11d-4; scan_hyp_outside.json r11o-3). Round 12: node-drop arms rechecked on x138 are negative in both embryos (I-147, I-148), and annotation density per predicted node is 0.79% on 44b6 vs 5.20% on 6bba, so node levers flip sign by embryo (research/wf_ideas_v4/round12/scan_recheck.json). Round 13: a free 1% node drop is worth +0.0009 (44b6 +0.0006) and a random 1% drop costs ~21x that; break-even enrichment r* ~0.03-0.05 is roughly density-free; the only existing node knob that pays is lp30 (probe 40); best in-lab pruning +0.0015 lab, far below R8 (research/wf_ideas_v4/round13/scan_hyp_node_count.json R13NC-1..7).

## TESTSET - Test-set structure / transductive / rules-policy ideas (overlap-consensus line)

Impact: Overlap consensus was re-opened as rule-legal (CONTEXT.md l.2096-2109) with a design ceiling of +0.004..+0.006 on a 44b6-type embryo and 0 on a 6bba-type one, then STOPPED by its own pre-registered G-A gate at 10:52 UTC (43/62 < 60) (CONTEXT.md l.2177-2186); the post-stop exp64 G-B read on the real K2 stack is -0.00143 (bootstrap lower -0.00200), confirming the stop (CONTEXT.md l.2198-2204); evidence cherry-picked into main, exp65/exp66 NEVER_PUSH (CONTEXT.md l.2220-2229). Public-recording matching (I-209) is closed on reach, not policy (round 10). Nothing live remains in this cluster.
